跨國鎮壓 / Transnational Repression

Tier E event record. The community is a party to this: people of Taiwanese and Hong Kong origin living in the United States, Canada and Europe are surveilled, coerced, or attacked on behalf of the PRC state. This page is not commentary on the news — it is the archive’s own record of a pattern we have documented since 2021, assembled from our own holdings so that any future story lands on a spine we already have.

從每日脈絡來的話

這頁不是今天的新聞。每日脈絡把你送到這裡，是因為今天的報導用了「跨國鎮壓」。點進來應看到兩件事：我們從 2021 年就有的紀錄，以及今天這幾則報導各自落在哪個已有的形狀上。下面每一則今天的消息都標 報導，不是我們核對過的檔案。

Labeling: 「跨國鎮壓」「代表中國警方」「統戰部」「受北京警方指示」are the prosecution’s and the papers’ framings carried as reported; all four are 嫌犯/被控, the case is 偵結 not adjudicated — defendants presumed innocent, nothing here is a finding of fact by this archive; 「李穎」as the account owner is the papers’ identification, our own page still holds the account identity as unknown (its NEED YOU line).

Focus Taiwan / CNA English, bylined (By Sean Lin), 發布 10/29/2026 07:15 PM (Taipei), dateline 「Taipei, Oct. 29 (CNA)」. Body read in full this run — the Google wire redirect again returned “Content was inaccessible or not found” to web_extract and jina 403’d on news.google.com (the t_88e678ef read-text rule); the canonical Focus Taiwan URL was fetched directly and the full article body read, including the sibling inline link 「● Five indicted over staged collision involving comedian critical of Xi」. Form matters for the ruling: this is the Cabinet-legislative leg of the 10-27→10-29 跨國鎮壓 cycle — the Executive Yuan announcing it is considering amendments to the Anti-Infiltration Act — not a new repression event against our people. Substance retained, all of it the Cabinet’s and the paper’s as reported: (1) 內閣（政院）發言人 Michelle Lee（李慧芝） at the weekly Cabinet news conference: the government is considering legislation to penalize individuals who engage in 「transnational repression」 in Taiwan on behalf of an 「external hostile force」; (2) the instrument is amendments to the Anti-Infiltration Act（反滲透法） establishing penalties for such conduct; (3) conduct that could constitute offenses under the proposed amendments: surveillance, harassment and intimidation; (4) 「We will use clearer, more specific rules to protect public safety in the future」 and 「Once the required legal procedures are complete, we will submit the draft amendments to the Legislative Yuan as soon as possible」— both Lee’s quoted words; (5) the trigger named by the paper: a reporter’s question about attempts backed by the Chinese government to pressure people in Taiwan into making political statements; (6) the paper’s own named example — entertainer Kevin Tsai（蔡康永） recently posted apologies on Weibo and RedNote (Xiaohongshu) after appearing at an event marking the opening of the campaign headquarters for Puma Shen（沈伯洋）, the DPP’s Taipei mayoral candidate; Tsai wrote 「I have always hoped that the two sides of the [Taiwan] Strait can interact amicably. I have never supported Taiwan independence」 and apologized to business partners and fans he may have disappointed; many in Taiwan interpreted the posts as apologies made under pressure from China; Tsai has not commented on whether he made the posts under duress (the paper’s own hedge, carried as its labeling); (7) the paper’s context lines: Shen is an expert on China’s cognitive warfare and disinformation campaigns and is among 14 individuals, most DPP-affiliated, whom the CCP has designated 「die-hard Taiwan independence separatists」; and the 2016 comparison case — Taiwanese K-pop idol Chou Tzu-yu（周子瑜） of Twice issued a recorded apology after briefly waving Taiwanese and South Korean flags on a South Korean TV show, saying she was 「proud to be Chinese」. Labeling: 「transnational repression」「external hostile force」「pressure people in Taiwan into making political statements」are the Cabinet’s and the paper’s framings carried as reported; 「many in Taiwan interpreted Tsai’s posts as apologies made under pressure」is the paper’s characterization of public reading, not a finding, and the paper itself records that Tsai never said he acted under duress; the 「die-hard」 designation is the CCP’s. Nothing here is a finding of fact by this archive.

Form matters for the ruling: this is the Canadian legislative-progress + cross-party-endorsement leg of the C-219 cycle already on this spine, not a new repression event against our people. Substance retained, all of it the paper’s and the quoted MPs’ as reported: (1) C-219《國際反腐敗與人權法案》（亦稱《謝爾蓋·馬格尼茨基法第三號》）is Canada’s first law to write 「跨國鎮壓」 and 「良心犯」 into statute; passed 眾議院 and is now in 參議院二讀; (2) sponsor James Bezan 貝贊（保守黨，影子國防部長，2025-09-16 提出）三讀辯論 2026-10-08: the bill renames 《腐敗外國官員受害者正義法》→《謝爾蓋·馬格尼茨基法》、《特別經濟措施法》→《全球制裁與特別經濟措施法》（第二號）, first defines 良心犯 and 跨國鎮壓 in Canadian law, requires 外交部長 annual 國會報告 on 人權捍衛者/良心犯 work, raises 規避制裁罰款上限 to $100,000, and sets a 24-month timetable for confiscation of sanctioned persons’ Canadian assets, proceeds distributable to victims or government; (3) Bezan names 法輪功學員 as victims of 騷擾、威脅、襲擊及活摘器官 — he says he received a letter the week before the debate from a 多倫多法輪功修煉者 assaulted and death-threatened while protesting peacefully outside the 中共領事館, argues the attackers may hide behind 外交豁免權 and 「必須為此接受審判」; answering Ziad Aboultaif 齊亞德·阿布勒泰夫（保守黨）he said the 法輪功 group 「受到迫害和殺害，其器官被非法摘取並出售到世界各地」; answering Mario Beaulieu 馬里奧·博利厄（魁人政團）he cited evidence of 醫生 and others trading in victims’ 心臟、肺、肝、腎（「一種令人作嘔的行為」）and criticized 中共對維吾爾人 實施「再教育」、絕育及強迫勞動; he calls the amended bill less comprehensive than the original but 「對於未來現代化加拿大制裁機制至關重要」, and urges an 國際反腐敗法院; (4) Bezan names 壹傳媒創辦人 黎智英 as the typical 良心犯（「他是一名良心犯，我們必須營救他」，per Bezan 已判終身監禁）, and alleges 北京 has 警察站 in Canada and 至少干預過兩次選舉; (5) cross-party endorsements: 自由黨資深議員 Judy Sgro 思格若（10-20 對大紀元：「真正開始讓這些法律長出牙齒 put some teeth in these laws」）、外交部長國會祕書 Mona Fortier 莫娜·福捷（「提高透明度，並使加拿大的制裁制度現代化」；委員會修訂使年度人權報告不再公開列名個別良心犯以贯彻「不造成傷害」原則）、Chi Nguyen 阮芝（「加強加拿大的制裁制度，並更新人權框架」，選區居民關注海外親友所受政治鎮壓與任意拘留）、Sameer Zuberi 薩米爾·祖貝里（委員會曾聽取 藏人、維吾爾人、法輪功修煉者、香港人及受伊朗、俄羅斯鎮壓者證詞）、魁人政團 博利厄（支持擴大制裁、限制受制裁者家屬簽證、RCMP–FINTRAC 合作及資產沒收）. Labeling: 「跨國鎮壓」「良心犯」「活摘」「警察站」「干預兩次選舉」「再教育、絕育及強迫勞動」are Bezan’s and the paper’s framings carried as reported; the 多倫多領館外襲擊信 is Bezan’s account of a letter he received (no police charge or court finding named in this copy); the 黎智英 終身監禁 characterization is Bezan’s/the paper’s; nothing here is a finding of fact by this archive.

2026-10-13 — 報導，尚未成為我們的檔案

報導。 邱垂正把北京制裁台灣友邦的現任部長，稱為新型態跨國鎮壓，美國國務院公開反對這項脅迫。檔案裡已有他以陸委會副主委身分談反滲透法的發言（陸委會：紅色滲透媒體須另檢討），沒有這次制裁的文件。形狀接近 2024 年已入檔的許穎婷遭懸賞：公開人物因為觸怒北京而被追究。證據等級不同。

報導。 卓榮泰就中國對台灣人跨境鎮壓、修反滲透法發言。檔案裡有他 2019 年在台大反送中之夜的紀錄（挺台大人反送中），沒有這次修法。

報導。 李明哲在歐洲巡講，提出民主國家是否該以「有罪推定」防範跨國鎮壓。檔案裡已有他的案子（台灣救不了李明哲）。巡講是新的，人不是。

Every characterization above is the speaker’s, attributed: no indictment, filing, or court finding is cited, and nothing here is recorded as established fact. Wanying Zhang, aka “Heather” (18 U.S.C. § 951), already carried under t_83a964c2 (2026-10-14), t_7979cbe0 (10-17), t_bca025f2 + t_35ee8f07 (10-19), t_2a804da6 (10-20), t_928b9374 (10-20). What the item alleges (all attributed; no finding of fact). 主席 界立建 says that around the September 2026 visit to the U.S. by 習近平 and the organization’s Washington protest, the alliance holds evidence of 20+ cases of intimidation, threats and harassment against members and their families; some were threatened before departure, gave up protesting, and were harassed anyway. Named first-person accounts: 徐其仁 (江西) supplied a call recording in which local 國保 personnel, having taken his mother to a 中共社區機構 and phoning him in front of her, said they knew of his CDPIA membership and his dealings with 界立建, demanded he withdraw, referenced his pregnant wife, warned his mother could be implicated and that he faced imprisonment if he returned to China. 張劉敏 (河南鄭州) and 方碩 (江蘇) each received threat calls before the Washington trip — 張劉敏 threatened with her child’s safety — both stood down, were harassed regardless, and were told to leave the alliance and cut contact with 界立建 or 「後果自負」; 張劉敏 said she would not withdraw and would publicize the case and pursue legal accountability. 界立建 said the harassment is not confined to families in China: the alliance’s Malaysia web administrator 楊佳 was followed and threatened and dare not return to his residence, and members in Thailand feared rendition to China. Rally attendee 李中平 said Flushing was chosen precisely because it is 華人集中, and called on the U.S. government to investigate CCP agents operating illegally in the U.S. and to protect threatened people. 界立建 said the alliance is collecting names, recordings and video to submit to the U.S. government, Congress and international human-rights bodies under the Global Magnitsky Act, and urged targets to report and preserve evidence.

Why this belongs in Echopedia

Per CONTROL §1b, the Tier D↔E discriminator is whether the community is a party, not whether the story is famous. It is:

  • The targets are members of, or adjacent to, the diaspora we record — 台灣人 abroad, 僑界, Hong Kong residents in U.S. cities, and family members of public figures.
  • The operations occur on soil where our members live: Los Angeles, the San Gabriel Valley, Toronto, university towns across the U.S.
  • Our archive carried the evidence before the current cycle made it a headline.

Labeling standard: every claim below carries its status. Sourced = a document in this archive. Reported = press assertion we have not independently verified. Alleged = accusation in a filing or statement, recorded with attribution and without a finding of fact. Nothing is omitted for being uncomfortable; nothing is asserted beyond what the record holds.

The pattern, as our own records show it
